Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: 1. Sample_rw.exe (PID 4011) runs from cmd.exe (E02). 2. File encryption *.locked and deletion (E03). 3. Ransom note placement (E04). 4. vssadmin.exe attempt (E05). 5. Registry key creation (E06). 6. No DNS/TCP (E07). Unique defects: 1. Missing hash in golden-14 (E01). 2. Inconsistent timeline timestamps (golden-10 vs golden-9). 3. Placeholder in golden-13 (E08). 4. Unresolved evidence reference in golden-12 (E06). 5. Contradiction in golden-12 (successful vssadmin vs golden-11 failure).
